([louiskeep/dev-loop](https://github.com/louiskeep/dev-loop/tree/HEAD/skills/conducting-the-loop))

([hopchouinard/Training-Generator](https://github.com/hopchouinard/Training-Generator/tree/HEAD/templates/training-repo/skills/conducting-training))
